Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the radioactivity object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 231. The moduli space has dimension at most 627 with 4 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on radioactivity structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 231. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 627. QED.
